47r APPENDIX I. KOWLOON AND LAPPA: QUARTERLY RETURNS OF TRADE. OOTOBER-DEOEMBER 1896. 23 *_
8 CUTOMS GAZ[TTE. OCT.-DEC., (Beceived x9th January 1897.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, 14TH JANUARY 1897. I.-4. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit S the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring ations in Native vessels for the fourth quarter of the year 1896, October-December. b. The Custom House (I ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, began on that day. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (1k.Ta 30 and Hk.Tn 8o per picul) commenced on the 14th April 1887, and the collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Oustom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, on the Ist July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are known as the Capsuimoon ( * ), Changehow (f 0%), Fotochow 0 VN, and Kowloon ( rs ) stations. c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II. MOVEMENTS OF VESSELS.-The number and movements of vessels, inwards and outwards, during the quarter, are set forth in the following Tables:- 10. CAPSUIMOON. JUNKS. OCTOBER. NOVEMBER. DECEMBER. TOTAL. 1896.] 1&quot;PAt (Raceived 19th Jaary 18 ) .CUSTOM H0USEt ILAPPA, grH JANUAR 1897. SIR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in yor Circular No. 381, Second Series, I nowtransmi to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarte of the year 1896, October-December. b. The Custom House ( 5) was opened on the 2nd April 1887. The collection of Tariff Import Dut and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.T2 30 and Hk.Th 8o per pioul) and the dollection of Provincial Likin and Ching-f Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, com menced on that day. The collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, commenced on the ist July 1887. The stations at present contiolled by the Lappa Customs are known as the Malowchow (PI &#39;S ) and Chienshan (&quot;ji ID) stations, with three sub-stations known as the Shekkok (; J), Kuttai ( ) and Kwanchiap (U f) sub-stations. c. The word &quot;Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
